### Source-backed relationship proposals in Raw-to-Wiki (2026-11-02; unreleased working tree)

Raw-to-Wiki can now offer typed relationship proposals alongside entity proposals. For the selected source only, BrainLink passes the local model at most 12 current, visible, non-private typed records whose titles occur in the redacted source. A candidate must resolve to unique current endpoints, use compatible entity types and an allowlisted predicate, stay within one scope, and cite an exact quote from the source. The user must select candidates; BrainLink saves them with `review: proposed` and a source link, while the existing graph queue remains responsible for approval. Scheduled auto-file does not create graph proposals. The regression test checks that the model sees only the selected source's named entities and that the saved proposal remains unverified and appears in the graph review queue.

Windows/Node.js 24.22.1 `npm run test:all` passed on this working tree: core 631 total (623 passed, 0 failed, 8 platform/privilege skips); UI 44/44. Local automated test evidence only. Live Ollama quality, GUI acceptance, hosted GitHub Actions, cross-client acceptance, and customer acceptance are not verified by this run. The proposal logic does not infer links to entities absent from the source, and image-only sources do not produce evidence-backed links.

### Fix manual Raw-to-Wiki scan button eligibility (2026-11-03; commit `2a53005`)

The Setup page previously re-enabled **Scan now** whenever any public vault existed, even if the user had not selected a scan folder. The button now uses one shared eligibility check: at least one selected folder must belong to an existing, non-private vault, and a scan must not already be running. The folder-selection render and state refresh both use this check.

Focused `test/ingestion-schedule.test.mjs` passed 5/5; full Windows/Node.js 24.22.1 `npm run test:all` passed on the exact pushed commit: core 638 total (628 passed, 0 failed, 10 platform/privilege skips), UI 44/44. The fix prevents an avoidable invalid manual-scan request; it does not change background scan behavior. Native visual interaction and customer acceptance remain unverified.

### Protect an existing macOS CLI command during package install (2026-11-02; working tree based on `6d9e13f`)

The macOS package post-install no longer uses `ln -sf` for `/usr/local/bin/brainlink`. A bundled helper makes the link only when the path is free or already targets this BrainLink app, and refuses to overwrite unrelated files or symlinks. The helper is installed executable in the app bundle. `.gitattributes` now forces LF for extensionless macOS package scripts; a Windows checkout had converted `postinstall` to CRLF, which fails Bash parsing when mounted into Linux. In Node.js 24 Debian Bookworm Docker, `bash -n` and the helper behavior tests passed 3/3, including idempotent reinstall and preservation of unrelated files and links. The full Windows/Node.js 24.22.1 `npm run test:all` passed on the working tree: core 634 total (624 passed, 0 failed, 10 platform/privilege skips); UI 44/44. This does not verify a native macOS `.pkg` build, install, upgrade or uninstall.

### Verify RSI promotion and rollback commit objects locally (2026-11-03; commit `a07032d`)

RSI acceptance previously checked that a promotion SHA had 40 hexadecimal characters and matched the recorded experiment, but did not check that the commit existed. Setup now verifies the promotion commit object in the local BrainLink Git repository before accepting an idea; a rollback receipt similarly requires the restored commit object. Missing repositories or commit objects fail closed. This is a local object check only: it does not query GitHub, hosted CI, deployment state, or independently confirm the human review. A packaged installation without a Git checkout cannot accept promotions or record rollback receipts. The Setup hints explain this in English, Portuguese and Traditional Chinese.

Focused `test/git-source-state.test.mjs` and `test/rsi-ledger.test.mjs` passed 13/13. No hosted CI, installed-package behavior, remote GitHub presence, or signed release was verified by these tests.

### Reject duplicate graph IDs in the development preview Notes editor (2026-11-03; working tree based on `c3ab004`)

The preview Notes editor now checks for an existing same-vault stable ID before saving graph metadata. The check reads candidate note details at save time because list summaries do not carry frontmatter; it ignores explicitly private notes and only blocks the write, leaving the note unchanged. Focused `ui/app/test/m2.test.mjs` passed 20/20, and full Windows/Node.js 24.22.1 `npm run test:all` passed: core 641 total (631 passed, 0 failed, 10 platform/privilege skips); preview UI 46/46. ### Installed graph relationship editing and reversible removal (2026-11-03; working tree after `367bb49`)

The installed Setup graph panel can edit a saved relationship's predicate, target, exact source excerpt, and confidence. The server checks the current note revision, visible same-scope target, matching entity types, and exact quote before saving. If an edge's meaning or evidence changes, it returns to `review: proposed` and leaves graph retrieval until reviewed again. Users can also remove an active or rejected edge from graph view while keeping its evidence, then restore it to review. Activity records contain only action labels.

Focused Windows/Node.js 24.22.1 graph checks passed 45/45, including the final duplicate-field and duplicate-edge guards. Full `npm test` passed: 644 total, 634 passed, 0 failed, 10 platform/privilege skips. `npm run test:ui` passed 46/46, and locale JSON plus `git diff --check` passed. A real browser click-through, packaged installer, and customer usability remain unverified. Revert the feature commit to roll back; no vault migration is required.

### Optional local search reranking (2026-11-03; working tree based on `b3346a3`)

Setup now has an opt-in local reranker URL, a connection check, and settings persistence. Only loopback HTTP endpoints are allowed. BrainLink reranks at most 20 already-authorized candidates using titles, headings, and short snippets; exact note links and explicit tag results remain pinned, and any error falls back to normal search. The reranker does not download a model. `npm run test:all` passed on the implementation before the final default-path adjustment: core 654 total (644 passed, 0 failed, 10 Windows privilege/platform skips), UI 48/48. After changing the default to llama.cpp's documented `/v1/rerank` route, the focused reranker, API, and locale checks passed 11/11; `git diff --check` passed. Tests cover unauthenticated rejection, safe setting persistence, remote-host refusal, and a fictional private-note canary absent from the reranker request. The setup is documented in `docs/LOCAL-RERANKING.md`. No real reranking service or model is installed here, so actual runtime compatibility, ranking quality, retrieval improvement, and token savings are unverified.
